Sphere Cap Ablation on Original LLM-JEPA

Matched-seed cap-anchor ablations layered on top of the reproduced Llama-3.2-1B original LLM-JEPA protocol. The primary metric is strict exact-match regex generation; geometry plots are diagnostic and do not replace downstream exact match.

Research Questions

IDQuestionPrimary contrastDecision rule
rq1Does the spherical cap loss improve the already-replicated original LLM-JEPA baseline?Best completed cap variant minus original LLM-JEPA on matched seeds.Treat as promising only if the paired mean delta is positive and at least four of five seeds are non-negative.
rq2Does spherical noise help independently of anchor/view choice?Noisy minus clean within the same frozen cap family: own_d1-own_d0, cross_d1-cross_d0, both_d1-both_d0.Noise is supported only by a positive paired mean in at least two families or one family with seed-level consistency.
rq3Is the useful target an own-view reconstruction, a cross-view prediction, or both?cross_d1-own_d1 and both_d1-best(own_d1,cross_d1) on matched seeds.Prefer the simplest view target that improves exact match without hurting seed consistency.
rq4Do moving anchors avoid over-constraining generation, or do fixed anchors provide the better signal?c_own_d1-own_d1 and ema_own_d1-own_d1.Moving anchors are useful only if they recover exact match while preserving a positive or neutral cap effect.
rq5If cap variants underperform, is the failure due to cap-loss weight/schedule conflict rather than the spherical idea?Follow-up lambda/schedule sweep for the least-bad cap family.Run lambda_cap in {0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5} and a delayed-cap schedule before rejecting the mechanism.

Current Interpretation

  • Original LLM-JEPA is the replication gate: it is 18.25 pp over regular SFT on completed seeds.
  • Best completed cap variant so far is Co-trained Own D1 lambda 0.05 at 73.09%, with matched-seed delta 1.32 pp versus original LLM-JEPA.
  • Under the 4-of-5 seed-consistency rule, these cap variants can no longer pass on exact match: Own D0, Own D1, Cross D0, Cross D1, Both D0, Both D1, C Own, EMA Own, C Own D0 .05.
  • Own-view noise paired delta is -4.70 pp over 3 completed seeds.
  • Cross-view noise paired delta is 3.25 pp over 3 completed seeds.
  • Own+cross noise paired delta is -1.38 pp over 3 completed seeds.
  • Low-dose co-trained noise paired delta is 0.04 pp over 5 completed seeds.

Next Airtight Experiments

PriorityNameQuestionDesignSuccess rule
mediumLambda-cap dose responseIs the cap loss useful only below lambda_cap=1.0?Run Co-trained Own D1 lambda 0.05 at lambda_cap in {0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5} with the same five seeds and the same original LLM-JEPA protocol.A setting is credible only if its matched-seed delta versus original LLM-JEPA is non-negative and not driven by one seed.
mediumDelayed or annealed cap scheduleDoes the cap objective conflict with early language-model adaptation?Hold lambda_cap=0 for epoch 1, then ramp to the selected lambda over epochs 2-4; compare against constant lambda on the same seeds.Schedule helps only if it improves exact match without erasing the noise/control contrast.
highGeometry versus generation diagnosticAre cap variants improving hidden-state geometry while hurting regex generation?For SFT, original LLM-JEPA, and the best cap variant, extract text/code hidden states for a fixed 512-example test subset and plot PCA, cosine histograms, retrieval heatmaps, RankMe, and paired-vs-unpaired cosine.A geometry-only win is not sufficient; it just tells us whether the objective is doing the intended representation work.
mediumCross-view symmetry ablationIs code-to-text reconstruction helpful or just extra pressure?If cross_d1 beats own_d1, run a cross symmetric variant and compare text-to-code only versus text-to-code plus code-to-text.Keep symmetry only if exact match and seed consistency improve over the one-way cross variant.
